- [x] **Six-month demo data generation** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-16-six-month-demo-data-design.md`, approach B). New `apps/backend/src/demo-data/` module: a chronological, stock-aware simulator (`generator/simulate.ts` + `generator/write.ts`) walks `today−6mo → today` day by day, holding in-memory stock per product/warehouse and driving the **real** `applyInventoryMovement` + `autoPostFromRules` primitives with explicit backdated dates — so the trial balance balances because it is *derived* from the tenant's own `PostingRule`s, not asserted. Full CLI run (`npm run seed:demo`) verified end-to-end against a real DB: ~2,972 sales / 187 purchases / 364 cashier sessions / returns / transfers / shrinkage / 2 quarterly stock-takes across six backdated months in ~19s, Σdebits == Σcredits == 5,395,725.00 (difference 0.00), vouchers dated 01-16…07-16 (not "today"). **Production primitive change:** added optional `occurredAt?: Date` to `applyInventoryMovement` (`inventory.utils.ts`) to backdate `InventoryMovement.created_at` — additive, no existing caller passes it. **New `DemoDataBatch` model** + migration `20260717120000_add_demo_data_batch`; in-process background execution with DB-backed progress: `POST /tenants/demo-data` → 202 + `{batchId,batchNumber}`, poll `GET /tenants/demo-data/status`; 409 while RUNNING; OWNER-only; orphaned RUNNING batches marked FAILED on module init. **Package split:** deleted `seedTenantDemoData` from `packages/database` (barrel `index.ts` **and** compiled `index.js` **and** recompiled `seed-demo.js` in lockstep, plus the `database-exports.spec.ts` drift-guard list); trimmed `seedDemoAccount` to pure scaffolding and added the four `SHRINKAGE` reasons it was missing. **Four demo catalogs** (`generator/catalogs/`, ~44-52 SKUs each) for GROCERY/PHARMACY/SURGICAL_MEDICAL/COMPUTER_HARDWARE with sell+cost price, reorder level, popularity weight, real `unit_type`; SURGICAL_MEDICAL SKUs/names sourced from `templates/surgical-medical.json`; selection by `tenant.business_type`, GROCERY fallback. Seeds `ProductPrice` rows (cost non-null, effective before the earliest sale) so `unit_cost_at_sale` resolves and the P&L shows real margins. **Returns bug fixes (test-first):** `sales-returns.service.ts` now writes a `CustomerCreditTransaction` + reduces `due_balance` when returning a credit sale; `purchase-returns.service.ts` now writes a `SupplierCreditTransaction` + reduces `due_balance`; both now pass `referenceId: <row id>` (was the `RET-`/`PRET-` string) so movements trace to the return. **Frontend:** extracted `ConfirmDialog` to `@/components/ui` (optional `expected` → plain-confirm vs type-to-confirm, all labels i18n props); Load Demo Data now shows an append/first-load confirm, a phase label + progress bar while polling, and recovers from a 409; new i18n keys across en/bn/ms (catalog parity test green). **Tests:** `generator/rng.spec.ts` (PRNG determinism) + `test/demo-data.integration.spec.ts` (7 invariants: debits==credits, stock==Σmovements, customer/supplier due==signed ledger, dates in window, backdating regression). Full backend suite 98/98 suites / 1283 tests green; frontend `tsc` only the pre-existing unrelated errors. Follow-ups (supplier-payment posting, shrinkage unitCost, per-business-type smoke runs) tracked in the open section above — done 2026-07-17
- [x] **Accounting posting correctness — fabricated vouchers + dead rules** (SDD Tasks 1-10, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-16-accounting-posting-correctness-design.md`): derived `DEFAULT_POSTING_RULES` from the `(eventType, conditionKey, conditionValue)` tuples callers actually emit, not from `seed.ts` (whose rules were largely dead — `classifyPaymentMode` collapsed every wallet to `bank`, `sale_return`/`purchase` hardcoded `cash`/`credit`). **Deleted the `fund_movement`/`none` and `inventory_adjustment`/`none` fallback rules** — the fabricated-voucher bug: `autoPostFromRules` fell back to a `condition_key:'none'` rule, so every warehouse transfer posted Dr Main Bank/Cr Cash in Hand for a pure stock movement (`warehouse-transfers.service.ts:131`) and every shrinkage/stock-take variance credited Cash in Hand (`inventory-shrinkage.service.ts:69`, `stock-takes.service.ts:190`); under this system's **periodic inventory** model those events correctly post nothing. Added `Accounts Receivable` (1030) — without it `ensureCustomerPaymentPostingSetup` returned early and every customer payment silently posted nothing — plus `bKash Account` (1015), `Nagad Account` (1016), `Purchases` (5015), and moved `Loans Receivable` 1030→1035 to clear the code collision. Made `bootstrap-accounting.ts` the single source of truth (`seed.ts` no longer defines any accounting). Fixed `classifyPaymentMode` (wallets no longer collapse to `bank`, extracted to `apps/backend/src/sales/classify-payment-mode.ts`) and `sales-returns.service.ts` (no longer hardcodes `'cash'`, now follows the original payment method or credits AR), making 8 previously-dead rules reachable. Enforced fiscal period locks (`assertFiscalPeriodOpen`) across all 5 posting paths — `FiscalPeriod.is_locked` was previously read by nothing. Added the caller↔rule contract test (`posting-contract.ts` + `.spec.ts`) — the regression guard for this whole bug class. Added `repair-fabricated-vouchers.ts` — **the only thing that fixes existing production tenants**, since the bootstrap runs only at tenant creation; fingerprints fabricated vouchers by source type + exact account-name pair, dry-run prints a reviewable per-voucher report. Re-enabled the 3 spec suites CI was skipping (`bootstrap-accounting.spec.ts`, `accounting.controller.spec.ts`, `customers.service.spec.ts`) via `1dee4f0`, which also flipped `customers.service.spec.ts`'s `autoPostFromRules` mock default from `'posted'` to `'skipped'`. Sanctioned extra fix: ported `ensureInterBranchAccounts` to `bootstrap-accounting.js` — see the separate entry immediately below (the fifth instance of the `.ts`/`.js` drift bug, found and fixed as part of this same effort). Commits: `19ca62e`, `9bf84c0`, `9ffb067`, `da9396a`, `9f09a60`, `3d02888`, `711209d`, `b723752`, `ad95840`, `1dee4f0`. Full test suite green throughout (final: 97/97 suites); `tsc --noEmit` only the 3 known pre-existing unrelated errors. Follow-ups (account_id-based posting, perpetual inventory, purchase-return/payment gaps, running the repair script against prod) tracked under the still-open section above — done 2026-07-16

Ported the function (+ the `bootstrapDefaultAccountingForTenant` call to it) into the `.js` twin verbatim, added it to `module.exports`; verified `typeof require('./packages/database/index.js').ensureInterBranchAccounts` went from `undefined` to `function`. - [x] Sales entry follow-ups (3 reported issues) — (1) Restored the "New Sales Entry" quick action on the Dashboard: commit `1f111f4` had deleted it from `FrequentQuickLinks.tsx` while its message claimed "It's on dashboard", so once the sidebar submenu was dropped there was no dashboard entry point; regression test added. (2) Sales list "New Sales Entry" button now uses the canonical primary style (`bg-primary hover:bg-primary-hover text-white`) — it had inherited the old POS button's neutral gray. (3) ROOT CAUSE of "payment options don't come from Payment Methods": the settings form POSTed `type: 'CASH'/'MOBILE_WALLET'` while the backend DTO `@IsEnum(PaymentMethodType)` validates the enum *values* (`'Cash'`, `'Mobile Wallet'`, …), so with the global `ValidationPipe` every create 400'd — the `PaymentMethod` table was empty for ALL tenants, making the entry UI always fall back to generics and leaving Show-on-Entry/Serial inert. Fixed by moving `PaymentMethodType` into `@erp71/shared-types` as the single source of truth (backend DTO + settings form both consume it), re-keying `TYPE_TO_CANONICAL` to the stored values (legacy uppercase keys retained) so wallet payments stop misclassifying as cash, and adding `seedDefaultPaymentMethods` (1 Cash, 2 bKash, 3 Nagad, 4 Card, 5 Bank) to both tenant-provisioning paths + a prod backfill for existing tenants. Contract tests added so the form/DTO vocabularies can't drift again — done 2026-07-15
- [x] Server-pagination hotfix: Leads footer showed "of 10" (page row-count), not the real total 146, and page 2+ was unreachable (pageCount collapsed to 1). Root cause: the backend `TransformInterceptor` reshapes paginated results to `{ data: items, meta: { total, ... } }`, but `fetchWithAuth` unwraps only `.data` and drops `meta` — so the frontend never received the server total. Added reusable `fetchPaginated()` (reads `meta` back into `{ items, total, page, limit, pages }`); `getLeads` uses it; leads page reads the typed envelope. Regression tests in `api.test.ts` cover the `{data,meta}` shape + bare arrays. NOTE for the 16-page rollout: every migrated list page MUST fetch via `fetchPaginated`, not `fetchWithAuth`, or it will hit this same dropped-total bug — done 2026-07-14
- [x] Server-side pagination + sorting foundation + CRM Leads slice — root-caused the "Showing 1–10 of 100" (real total 146) bug: `DataTable` paginated purely client-side over a server-capped 100-row slice, so the count was wrong AND leads 101–146 were unreachable. Added opt-in `serverPagination` prop to `DataTable` (TanStack `manualPagination`/`manualSorting`, footer count from server `total`, page/size/sort routed through callbacks; client mode 100% unchanged), a reusable injection-safe `resolveOrderBy` allowlist helper (`apps/backend/src/common/sort.util.ts`), `sortBy`/`sortDir` on the leads list endpoint (allowlist: name/category/priority/status/score/next_step_date/created_at; unknown→existing default order), and wired the Leads page (page/pageSize/sort state, reads full `{items,total}` envelope, resets to page 1 on filter/sort change). TDD throughout (be 34/34, fe 44/44); per-task + whole-branch subagent reviews. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-server-pagination-design.md`; plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-14-server-pagination-foundation-leads.md` — done 2026-07-14

- [x] Fix signup `?plan=` query-param override race (code review findings) — `apps/frontend/src/app/signup/page.tsx` had two mount-time `useEffect`s that both set `form.planCode`: the async `getSignupDefaults()` effect and the sync `?plan=` query-param effect. Because the async effect resolved later, its `.then` always overwrote an explicit `?plan=basic` selection with the backend default. Fixed by having the default-plan effect re-check `searchParams.get('plan')` inside its `.then` (via `PLAN_QUERY_TO_CODE`/`isSelfServeSubscriptionPlan`/`isComingSoonSubscriptionPlan`) and skip applying the default when a valid `?plan=` selection is present; dependency array widened to `[searchParams]`. Added 2 tests to `page.test.tsx` (default-plan preselect with no `?plan=`; `?plan=basic` survives the async default resolving) — required making the `next/navigation` mock's `useSearchParams` per-test-configurable via a stable `URLSearchParams` reference, and adding `mockClear()` for the `api` mocks in `beforeEach` (they were never reset between tests, which had silently masked the race in an early draft of the new test). `npx jest src/app/signup/page.test.tsx` → 3/3 pass; `tsc --noEmit` unchanged (3 pre-existing unrelated errors). Commit `b4dd985` — done 2026-07-10

- [x] Fix toasts buried behind the floating feedback button — ROOT CAUSE (first diagnosis was wrong): the buried toasts are the ~20 per-page save/status toasts rendered inline at `fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50`, which share the exact corner AND z-index of the feedback dock (`FloatingAssistDock`, `fixed bottom-safe right-safe z-50`). With equal z-index, DOM order wins — the dock renders after `<main>` (layout.tsx:535) so the feedback button painted over the toast. Fix: lowered the dock to `z-40` so all z-50 bottom-right toasts (and the z-70 central Toaster) render above it; still above normal page content, now correctly below modals. One line in `FloatingAssistDock.tsx`. (Earlier `Toaster.tsx` `bottom-24`/`z-[70]`/`overflow-y-auto` change was aimed at the wrong component — the central top-right Toaster can't reach the button — left in place as harmless hardening.) — done 2026-07-11

- [x] CRM sidebar: "Leads" and "Campaigns" not showing on Standard plan — two distinct root causes. - [x] Team & Permissions role change didn't affect session/admin view (coarse enum desync, part 2) — the prior fix corrected `invitations.service.updateMemberRole` (`PATCH /invitations/members/:userId/role`), but the Team & Permissions UI actually calls `PATCH /team/members/:userId/role` → `TeamService.updateRole`, which wrote only `tenant_role_id`. So an owner changing Accountant→Manager left the coarse `TenantUser.role` enum stale, and the session + platform-admin Edit Tenant (both read the enum) kept showing the old role. Fixed `team.service.ts:updateRole` to set `role: resolveBaseUserRole(role.name)` in lockstep (it already fetched the role via getTenantRole; return value was discarded). Confirmed via grep this was the last remaining `tenantUser.update` write path missing the enum. The seed backfill from the earlier fix reconciles already-affected members on deploy. team.service test updated to assert the enum (13 pass) — done 2026-07-09
- [x] Store rename module (backend) — SDD task 4 of the simplified-signup/store-rename/default-plan plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-10-simplified-signup.md`). Added `MANAGE_STORES` to `StorePermission` in `packages/shared-types/index.ts` (plus its label + Administration group entry) and a new `stores` NestJS module: `StoresService.rename(tenantId, storeId, name)` (404s via `NotFoundException` if the store isn't in the tenant, trims the name) behind `PATCH /stores/:id`, guarded by `JwtAuthGuard` + `StorePermissionGuard`/`@RequireStorePermission(MANAGE_STORES)` + `TenantInterceptor`, mirroring `tenants.controller.ts`/`team.module.ts`. Since `StorePermission` is also a Postgres enum (`packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma`) consumed by `ROLE_DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS[OWNER]` in the demo seed, added migration `20260710120000_add_manage_stores_permission` (`ALTER TYPE "StorePermission" ADD VALUE`) — not called out in the task brief but required for `tsc`/seed to stay consistent; applied to the local dev DB via `prisma db push` + `prisma generate` (DB wasn't `migrate`-baselined locally, same known gap as Task 3). - [x] Fix duplicate migration timestamp collision — `20260710120000_add_manage_stores_permission` shared its timestamp prefix with `20260710120000_drop_user_mobile_unique`, breaking chronological migration ordering. Commit `8c9e652` — done 2026-07-10
- [x] Invited Manager shows as Cashier after login (coarse role enum desync) — root cause: the invitation-accept path hardcoded `TenantUser.role = CASHIER` while only the granular `tenant_role_id` reflected the invited role. The session/`request.userRole` (and OWNER/MANAGER auth gates) read the coarse enum, so an invited Manager displayed and *authorized* as Cashier; the Team screen reads `tenant_role_id → name` so it showed Manager — hence the disagreement. `updateMemberRole` had the same defect (only wrote `tenant_role_id`). Fix: new `resolveBaseUserRole(name)` + `SYSTEM_TENANT_ROLE_TO_USER_ROLE` in shared-types (system role name → UserRole, default CASHIER for custom roles); `grantMembershipTx` and `updateMemberRole` now set the coarse enum in lockstep; idempotent backfill added to `seed.ts` (which runs on every prod deploy via `db push` + `db:seed`) reconciling existing non-owner members on a system role. Permissions were already correct (granular userStorePermission rows). 78 invitations/team/auth tests pass (updated accept-signup + updateMemberRole assertions) — done 2026-07-08
- [x] Invitation accept for users without an account — new invitees could not accept: the accept page only offered "Sign in"/"Create account", and the generic `/signup` form (SignupDto requires tenantName/storeName) provisioned them a *new* org instead of joining the inviting tenant. Fixed by making `/accept-invitation` a combined form: `GET /invitations/info` now returns `hasAccount`; logged-out invitees with an existing account get an inline password sign-in, and brand-new invitees get an inline name+mobile+password form. New public `POST /invitations/accept-signup` creates the user (email taken from the invitation token, email marked verified since the token proves ownership) and joins them to the tenant atomically, reusing an extracted `grantMembershipTx` helper shared with `accept()`; the page then logs them in via the existing login. i18n added for en/bn/ms. 15 invitations service tests pass (3 new: hasAccount reporting, accept-signup happy path, duplicate-email rejection) — done 2026-07-08
- [x] Sidebar: collapse all menus by default on login + accordion navigation (feedback cmra8ve0) — submenus already defaulted collapsed, but persisted `localStorage` state carried expanded menus across logins. Reworked into an accordion: added pure `accordionOpenState`/`accordionCloseState` helpers (`sidebar-nav-filter.ts`); `toggleGroup` now opens one section's ancestor chain and closes all others; the active-route effect *replaces* (was *merges*) open state with the current page's chain, guarded by a `lastAutoOpenPathRef` so re-renders that rebuild `modules` (locale switch, async nav-layout load, permission change) don't clobber a manual choice; and login (`storeAuthResponse`) clears `sidebar-open-groups`/`sidebar-collapsed`/`sidebar-width` via new `clearSidebarLayoutState()`, so the dashboard landing (no group) loads fully collapsed. Expand-all/Collapse-all kept as manual overrides; signup out of scope. Spec + plan in `docs/superpowers/`; 29 Sidebar/nav tests + 3 auth-session tests pass — done 2026-07-08
- [x] Fix feedback-automation implement step producing no file changes across multiple models — the write-tool wiring was correct, but the implement prompt was soft ("write_file for every file you add or change… then respond with a summary (no tool call)"), so mid models (deepseek, gemini-2.5-flash) satisfied it by describing the change in prose and never calling write_file. Fixes: (a) rewrote buildImplementSystemPrompt to forcefully require applying edits via write_file with explicit path/content args and "prose is a failed run"; (b) added a no-writes nudge in runToolLoop — if the model tries to finish (no tool calls) in implement mode without having written anything, it's told to actually call write_file (up to 2×) before the run is accepted; plan mode never nudges. Verified nudge control-flow (no-writes→nudge→recover, writes→no-nudge, plan→no-nudge); 38 tests pass — done 2026-07-08
- [x] Fix feedback-automation implement step failing with opaque "Command failed: git ... commit" — when the model returns a summary without calling write_file, the working tree stays clean and `git commit` errors ("nothing to commit" → stdout, so the stderr-based handler showed only the raw command). `runImplementation` now guards on `result.filesChanged.length === 0` and throws an actionable message (retry / switch to a stronger model) before commitAndPush. Root cause was the model choice (deepseek-v4-pro unreliable at tool-calling); the fix improves the error, the real remedy is a strong tool-calling model. Added empty-change-set test (27 pass). Deployed d6acaee — done 2026-07-08

Deploy convention saved to memory — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Fix platform admin tenant-features page load + Save Settings errors — root cause: the page (and its siblings) were written against the old `fetchWithAuth` contract where it returned a raw `Response`, calling `.then(r => r.json())` on load and checking `if (!res.ok)` on save. Since commit `c234bc0` (auth hardening) `fetchWithAuth` unwraps `{ data }` and returns parsed data, so `r.json()` threw a TypeError (→ loadFailed toast) and `!res.ok` was always truthy (→ "Save failed" toast). Fixed the reported `tenant-features` page plus every other page carrying the same misuse: `general`, `ai`, `email`, `sms`, `whatsapp`, `payments` under admin/platform-settings, and tenant `settings/sms`, `settings/tax`, `settings/reports` — including the `/test` handlers in ai/email/sms/whatsapp that also read `.json()`/`.ok`. Also fixed the `email/page.test.tsx` mocks, which had been asserting the *wrong* (`{ ok, json }`) contract and were exactly why the bug slipped past CI — now mock the unwrapped return value. Verified: 106/106 platform-settings + settings tests green, edited files typecheck clean, and a full-frontend grep confirms zero `.json()`/`.ok`-on-fetchWithAuth misuse remains — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Fix "Remember Me" login persistence (feedback cmr81ywm, PR #333) — wired the previously-inert checkbox to a `rememberMe` state and threaded it through `storeAuthResponse` so an unchecked login stores the token in `sessionStorage` (cleared on browser close) and a checked login persists it in `localStorage`; token/context readers (`getAccessToken` in `api.ts`, `getStorage` in `auth-session.ts`) now fall back across both backends and `clearAuthSession` clears both. Also repaired the PR's two regressions: the entire `export const api = {...}` block (~1920 lines) had been deleted from `apps/frontend/src/lib/api.ts` (restored from `dev` + re-applied the token change) and the login email placeholder had been corrupted to `[EMAIL]` (reverted to `name@company.com`); demo login now persists via `storeAuthResponse(auth, true)`. Full frontend suite green (1444 tests) — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Feedback automation: in-app "Merge PR" for platform admins — while a feedback item is PR_OPENED the panel now polls CI + mergeability and shows live status; a Merge PR button enables only when all checks passed (≥1 present) AND GitHub reports the PR mergeable (no conflicts), then merges via merge commit and advances to MERGED. Backend re-verifies the green gate server-side before merging (never trusts the button). New `getPrReadiness`/`mergePullRequest`/`computePrGreen` in feedback-github.service, `mergeFeedbackPr` + extended `refreshPrStatus` in the service, `POST admin/feedback/:id/merge` endpoint, panel Merge button + PR_OPENED auto-poll, unit/service tests. Design spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-07-feedback-pr-merge-design.md — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Fix nightly read-only E2E smoke failures (6 failed on 2026-07-07 run) — root cause was the `hub-navigation` card selector going stale after commit 8658b68 ("Roll out compact UI density") migrated hub cards to `ModuleHub`→`CompactLinkGrid`, whose title span is `text-sm font-semibold` (no heading), while the test still matched only `h2, h3, .text-sm.font-bold` → zero cards on /sales, /purchases, /inventory. Those failures cascaded into 429s: Playwright restarts the worker after each failure, resetting the module-level session cache and forcing a fresh `/auth/login` each time, which exhausted the 10/60s login throttle (hitting HR/POS/Dashboard). Fixes: (a) widened the hub-card selector to also match `.text-sm.font-semibold`; (b) added `loginWithThrottleRetry` back-off to `fetchE2ESession` (mirrors `demo-auth`'s `fetchWithThrottleRetry`) so transient 429s no longer hard-fail login. Verified selector against `CompactLinkGrid` DOM + `playwright test --list` parses — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Fix feedback-automation "Propose Plan" failing with "terminated" — undici dropped the long-running OpenRouter socket as `TypeError: terminated`, and because the `response.json()` body read sat outside the try/catch it propagated bare into `lastError`. `callOpenRouter` now: reads the body inside a guarded scope; adds a 180s abort-based per-request timeout; retries transient failures (connection drop / timeout / 429 / 5xx) up to 3× with linear backoff; short-circuits non-retryable 4xx (bad key/model); and always surfaces a descriptive message instead of a bare error. Verified retry control flow (success/recover/exhaust/non-retryable) — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Fix feedback-automation "Propose Plan" failing with "Agent did not finish within 20 tool-call turns" — the hardcoded `MAX_TURNS = 20` in `feedback-agent-runner.service.ts` was too low for a monorepo this size, so read-only exploration exhausted the budget and threw. Now: (a) the final allowed turn withholds tools so the agent must return a best-effort plan instead of failing; (b) the budget is configurable via `feedback_automation.max_turns` (default 40, clamped 5–100) with a UI field; (c) `callOpenRouter` omits the `tools` field when empty. Verified clamp boundaries + loop-termination invariant — done 2026-07-07
- [x] Register "Feedback Automation" and "Add-on Modules" in the platform-admin sidebar nav — both pages existed as index-grid cards + standalone routes but were never added to `NAV_REGISTRY` / `DEFAULT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_NAV_LAYOUT`, so neither appeared in the sidebar menu (Feedback Automation was only reachable by direct URL). - [x] Fix New Sale reference-number collision on a new calendar day — `generateReferenceNumber` scoped existing refs to `created_at` today only, but the `YYMM-#` prefix is monthly (e.g. `2606-005` from yesterday still blocks `2606-001` today). Removed the date filter so the max sequence is taken across all refs with the current YYMM prefix; added `generateReferenceNumber()` spec; rebuilt local Docker backend; verified `POST /sales` returns 201 with `2606-006` — done 2026-06-23
- [x] Make New Sale entry actually create sales (3 bugs found by driving the UI end-to-end) — (1) **Product search 500** — Postgres `SUM()` returns a BigInt; `searchByQuantitySold()`'s `.sort((a,b)=>b.qty_sold-a.qty_sold)` threw `Cannot convert a BigInt value to a number`, so the dropdown was empty and no product could be added. Coerce `qty_sold` to `Number()` (`products.service.ts`); also coerce the serialized Decimal `price` to a number on the client (`ProductSearch.tsx`, `page.tsx`). (2) **Create Sale 400** — `CreateSaleDto`/nested DTOs had zero `class-validator` decorators, so the global `ValidationPipe({ whitelist, forbidNonWhitelisted })` rejected every property ("property X should not exist"). Decorated all sale DTOs (`sale.dto.ts`). Frontend also sent `storeId: currentUser?.store_id` which is undefined for owners → now reads the active branch from `localStorage['store_id']` like POS does (`page.tsx`). (3) **2nd-sale-of-the-day 500** — `generateReferenceNumber` filtered existing refs with `startsWith` on a prefix that still contained the literal `#` placeholder, so the count was always 0 and every auto-ref was `…-001` → `(tenant_id, reference_number)` unique-constraint collision. Rewrote to take the max existing sequence for the day's literal prefix and increment (`sales.service.ts`). Verified by driving the running Docker app with Playwright: 4 sales created (Cash/Card/Mobile Wallet), all 201/COMPLETED, accounting vouchers posted, persisted in the Sales list; updated `sales.service.spec.ts` tx mock (`sale.findMany`); products+sales specs 22/22 green — done 2026-06-22
- [x] New Sale UI review fixes — (1) the global floating Feedback widget was covering the Create Sale button: lifted the right-panel action bar above it (`lg:pb-16` desktop, `pb-20` mobile). (2) Payment methods now reflect the Payment Methods management definition: `PaymentSection` loads `api.getPaymentMethods()` and shows the tenant's *active* methods as quick-add chips (by their real names, sorted), auto-linking each method's account; inactive methods + the 4 generic types live behind an "Other ▾" menu; falls back to the generic 4 when none are defined. To keep accounting posting correct (backend classifies by substring on the method string), the submitted `method` stays canonical (Cash/Mobile Wallet/Card/Bank) derived from the method `type`, with a new optional `Payment.label` carrying the friendly name for display. (3) Mobile responsiveness: below `lg` the page now flows and scrolls naturally (the POS fixed-viewport/internal-scroll behavior applies only at `lg+`), and the action bar clears the fixed widgets — verified at 1440px (no overflow) and 390px (form scrolls, all controls reachable) — done 2026-06-22
- [x] Accounting-focused subscription pack — added a new `ACCOUNTING` plan code (between BASIC and STANDARD, ৳749/mo) targeting bookkeeping-only customers. Approach: "just the plan, gate nothing new" — no module gates were changed. The plan's `features_json` sets `premiumAccounting: true` (unlocks the feature-gated `/accounting` module), while expenses/payment-methods/cost-centers/loans are already ungated so they work on any active subscription. Changes: `SubscriptionPlanCode` enum + migration `20260620000000_add_accounting_plan`; `SubscriptionPlanCode`/`AI_CREDITS_PER_PLAN` in shared-types; `PLAN_RANK` (ACCOUNTING=0, so it never unlocks BASIC/STANDARD `@RequiresPlan` routes outside accounting scope) + `PlanCode` type in `subscription-access.guard.ts`; seed plan in `seed.ts`; widened `planCode` unions/`@IsIn` in billing/admin-tenants/auth DTOs + services so it's assignable & self-serve purchasable; frontend marketing plans + comparison column, signup/admin/billing plan selectors, en/bn/ms admin labels. Backend builds clean; frontend typechecks clean (only pre-existing `.test.tsx` errors remain) — done 2026-06-20
- [x] Redesign New Sale entry UI to be compact and fit on screen without scrolling — replaced the seven stacked `p-6` white cards (which forced page scrolling) with a POS-style full-height two-column layout mirroring `pos/page.tsx`: a slim top strip (back + title + inline Sales#/Ref#/User/Date meta), a left work area (inline card-less Customer search/chip + Product search, a flex-grow line-items table that is the *only* internal scroll region with a sticky header and denser rows — Product-ID column dropped, plus a one-line optional note field), and a fixed ~320px right panel holding the dense totals summary (discount %, transport, labor, rounding as inline inputs on their value rows), the narrow stacked Payment section, and Cancel/Print/Create actions pinned to the bottom. Below `lg` the panel stacks under the work area and the page may scroll (mobile). Pure presentation refactor of `sales/new/page.tsx` + its 6 components; no changes to `useNewSaleCart`, totals calc, validation, submit, or print. Typecheck clean for all sales/new files — done 2026-06-21
- [x] Get the E2E (Playwright) suite green — it had never actually run (the CI job never started the frontend), so the specs had drifted from the app. Fixes: started the frontend in the E2E workflow + waited on it; associated `<label>`s with inputs on login/signup (a11y; unblocks `getByLabel`); added a Playwright `globalSetup` that provisions the canonical E2E account via the signup API (idempotent + retry for the provisioning transaction-timeout); seeded subscription plans in the E2E DB (signup needs a valid plan); made the API throttler limit env-configurable and raised it for E2E (the sidebar route sweep was tripping 429s and cascading); aligned stale specs with real behaviour (native HTML5 validation, `/verify-email` redirect, unique `/your name/i` locators, server-side-only auth protection, disabled-but-present POS checkout button); fixed a real storefront-dashboard crash (`data.items` undefined → guard); unified E2E credentials. Result: full pipeline green — E2E 73 passed. PR #285. (Note: E2E does not gate Render deploys.) — done 2026-06-17
- [x] Fix backend Render deploy failing in pre-deploy DB sync — the New Sales feature added `@@unique([tenant_id, reference_number])` on `Sale`, and `prisma db push` aborts with "Use the --accept-data-loss flag" when adding a unique constraint. The Docker image builds/pushes fine; the deploy exited status 1 during the `preDeployCommand`. Added `--accept-data-loss` to both `prisma db push` invocations (render.yaml `preDeployCommand` + backend Dockerfile start CMD). Safe here: `reference_number` is a newly-added nullable column so all existing rows are NULL, and Postgres treats NULLs as distinct in unique indexes — no rows conflict, nothing is dropped — done 2026-06-17
- [x] Fix failing CI/CD pipeline that blocked Render deploys (backend + frontend) — Render gates deploys on the shared `CI/CD Pipeline` passing, so multiple test/build failures across the last two commits (`0d0ce5f` price lists, `468e54a` New Sales page) blocked **both** services. Root causes & fixes: (1) **Real backend build breakers** — `payment-methods` + `sales-settings` modules (controllers/services/modules) from `468e54a` were authored against non-existent conventions (`@/shared/...` path alias, `PrismaService`); rewrote them to repo conventions (`DatabaseService`, `../auth/jwt-auth.guard`, `../database/tenant.interceptor`, `Tenant`/`TenantContext` decorator with `@UseInterceptors(TenantInterceptor)`, `DatabaseModule`) — these `TS2307` errors would have failed `nest build` in the Docker image. (2) `products.service.spec.ts` missing new `PriceListsService` dep. (3) `sales.service.spec.ts` tx mock missing `salesSettings.findUnique` + `sale.count` (new reference-number generation) and a payment assertion missing `account_id`. (4) `auth.service.spec.ts` `db.account` mock missing `findFirst` (pre-existing break from the loan-posting `ensureLoanPostingSetup`). (5) Frontend `settings/page.test.tsx` lucide-react mock missing the new `ShoppingBag`/`CreditCard` icons. Verified: `nest build` clean, all 5 affected specs pass, full frontend suite 1290/1290, lint exit 0 — done 2026-06-17
- [x] Platform admin "Add New Tenant" (manual signup on behalf of a customer) — `POST /admin/tenants` + `GET /admin/users/lookup` on `AdminTenantsController`/`AdminUsersController` (both `JwtAuthGuard + PlatformAdminGuard`); `CreateAdminTenantDto` with `ownerMode: new|existing`. `AdminTenantsService.createTenant` provisions tenant + OWNER `TenantUser` + store + TRIALING 14-day subscription (chosen `planCode`) + `UserStoreAccess` + OWNER `UserStorePermission`s + default accounting in one `$transaction`, plus optional business-type template seed. New-user path creates the user inside the tx with a throwaway bcrypt hash (passwordHash is non-nullable) and a `PasswordResetToken`, then emails a reset link so the owner sets their own password; existing-user path reuses the looked-up user. Audit-logged as `tenant.admin_create`. Frontend: "New Tenant" button + create modal on `/dashboard/admin/tenants` (New/Existing-user tabs with email lookup, tenant/store/address/business-type/plan fields), `createAdminTenant`/`lookupAdminUser` API client methods, i18n en/bn/ms. Spec + plan in `docs/superpowers/` — done 2026-06-17
- [x] Basic loan management — track money the business has borrowed (PAYABLE) or lent out (RECEIVABLE) with repayment recording and outstanding-balance tracking. New `Loan` + `LoanPayment` Prisma models and migration `20260615000000_add_loans` (also adds `VIEW_LOANS`/`MANAGE_LOANS` to the `StorePermission` enum); `loans` NestJS module (controller/service/dto) registered in `app.module.ts` with full tenant-scoped CRUD, `POST /loans/:id/payments` (validates against outstanding, auto-closes a fully-repaid loan), `DELETE /loans/:id/payments/:paymentId` (re-opens), and `GET /loans/summary`. Added `VIEW_LOANS`/`MANAGE_LOANS` to shared-types permission matrix (labels, role defaults for OWNER/MANAGER/ACCOUNTANT, Accounting & Funds group). Frontend: `/dashboard/loans` page (summary cards, type/status filters, add/edit modal, detail modal with repayment ledger), API client methods, sidebar link under Accounting, and en/bn/ms localization — done 2026-06-15
- [x] Loan automatic journal postings — wired loans into the posting-rules engine. Added `loan_disbursement`/`loan_repayment` event types + a `loan_direction` condition key (schema enums, migration `20260616000000_add_loan_posting`, shared-types, posting DTO validation, `posting.utils` union + JOURNAL voucher mapping). Added `Loans Payable` (2020) and `Loans Receivable` (1030) accounts plus 4 default posting rules to the accounting bootstrap, exposed via an idempotent `ensureLoanPostingSetup` so existing tenants get them lazily on first loan activity. Creating a loan posts a disbursement voucher and recording a repayment posts a repayment voucher (correct Dr/Cr per direction), inside a transaction via the existing idempotent engine; posting-rules UI shows the new event/condition labels. Covered by `loans.service.spec.ts` + updated `bootstrap-accounting.spec.ts`. Known limitation: deleting a loan/payment does not auto-reverse its voucher (manual reversing entry, consistent with the rest of the system) — done 2026-06-16

- [x] Automatic journal postings for expense entries — added `expense` to `PostingRuleEventType` enum (schema + migration 13), wired `autoPostFromRules` into `ExpensesService.createEntry()` using a DB transaction with `payment_mode` condition (cash/bank), added default expense posting rules (debit Expense, credit Cash/Bank) to `bootstrapDefaultAccountingForTenant` — done 2026-06-16

- [x] Department & Designation management — dedicated UI pages at `/dashboard/employees/departments` and `/dashboard/employees/designations` with full create/edit/delete (soft-delete with employee assignment guard); backend PATCH/DELETE endpoints added to `EmployeesController`; sidebar links added; i18n for en/bn/ms; employee add/edit forms already had department/designation selects — done 2026-06-15

- [x] Platform Admin vs shop login separation + workspace chooser — platform admins no longer see shop owner/user sidebar options: introduced a "login context" concept (`active_context` localStorage = `platform-admin` for the admin console, otherwise a tenant). After login, `storeAuthResponse` resolves contexts: a single shop or admin-only goes straight in, but when more than one workspace exists (admin email that also owns a shop, or a user belonging to multiple shops) the user is sent to a new `/select-account` chooser to pick the Platform Admin Console or a specific shop. `Sidebar` gets `platformAdminMode` (renders only admin + help) and a "Switch account" link (`canSwitchAccount`); `dashboard/layout` derives admin mode, hides the branch selector / shop onboarding in admin mode, labels the user as "Platform Admin", and routes `/dashboard` → `/dashboard/admin`. Frontend-only — `getMe()` already returns `is_platform_admin` + `tenants[]` — done 2026-06-15

- [x] Fix production login 500 (full resolution) — root cause was Prisma schema drift on fresh DB: preDeployCommand (`prisma db push`) was silently failing because DIRECT_URL (external hostname) is blocked by Render's IP allowlist when connecting from within the container; fixed by overriding `DIRECT_URL=$DATABASE_URL` in Dockerfile CMD so prisma uses the internal Render hostname; also moved `npm run db:seed` into Dockerfile CMD so schema sync + seeding runs at startup (idempotent) before NestJS receives traffic; production DB was recreated fresh (old DB `dpg-d8kiaj6q1p3s73fkmum0-a` deleted, new DB `dpg-d8ndt967r5hc73anmdk0-a`) — done 2026-06-14

- [x] Fix demo login (`POST /auth/demo`) returning 500 in Docker — root cause: `packages/database/index.js` (stale committed compiled file) never exported `DEMO_ACCOUNT_EMAIL` from `seed-demo.ts`, so the constant was `undefined` in the running container; fixed by inlining the constant in `index.js` — done 2026-06-14

- [x] Nightly read-only E2E monitoring — every midnight Bangladesh time a scheduled GitHub Actions workflow (`.github/workflows/nightly-readonly-e2e.yml`, cron `0 18 * * *`) runs the `@readonly`-tagged Playwright suite against the live deployment (`https://erp71-frontend.onrender.com`) to confirm all feature routes and read-only flows are up. Tagged 69 read-only cases across `sidebar-routes`/`auth`/`billing`/`pos` specs and parameterized their base URLs + login via env (`PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL`, `E2E_API_URL`, `E2E_TEST_EMAIL/PASSWORD`); data-mutating cases (signup, complete-sale, critical-path) are excluded so prod data is never touched. `scripts/format-e2e-report.js` turns the Playwright JSON report into an email subject + HTML body that lists every failed case, sent to nayeem.ahmad@gmail.com via Brevo SMTP. Requires repo secrets (`E2E_TEST_EMAIL/PASSWORD`, `SMTP_HOST/PORT/USER/PASS`, `EMAIL_FROM`) — done 2026-06-14

- [x] Pre-populated products by business type (surgical/medical) — 1,173 real products from Care Force Medical PDF classified into 24 groups / 104 subgroups; `packages/database/prisma/templates/surgical-medical.json` (194KB) + `seed-template.ts` with `seedBusinessTypeTemplate(prisma, tenantId, businessType)`; `business_type` field added to Tenant schema (migration `20260614000000_add_business_type_to_tenant`); seeder auto-triggered in `setupTenant` after registration; onboarding UI shows 4 business type tiles (surgical/medical live, others "coming soon") — done 2026-06-14

- [x] Fix 12 failing nightly E2E tests — two root causes: (1) Auth heading mismatches: `auth.spec.ts` heading regexes updated to match actual i18n text ("Welcome back", "Create your ERP71 workspace", "Create workspace"); `loginIfNeeded()` in `billing.spec.ts` and `pos.spec.ts` rewritten to use URL check instead of heading detection, fixing silently-skipped login that caused billing/POS tests to fail; (2) Sidebar `/500/` false positive: the broad `/500/` pattern in `FATAL_PATTERNS` matched monetary values like ৳500 and loyalty points — changed to narrow `/500\s*\|\s*internal server error/i`; also fixed `selected_tenant_id`/`selected_store_id` localStorage keys to `tenant_id`/`store_id` matching what `fetchWithAuth` and `storeAuthResponse` actually use — done 2026-06-14

- [x] Utilize full content width on dashboard screens — removed centered max-width caps (`max-w-[1400px]`, `max-w-6xl/5xl/4xl`, `max-w-[1000px]/[1100px]`) on 58 table/report/dashboard pages so they fill the available width to the right of the sidebar (the outer `p-6` wrapper keeps margins); single-column forms (settings, platform-settings), detail/invoice documents, and print pages intentionally left at a readable width — done 2026-06-14
- [x] Prepaid SMS credit balance & top-up — shops buy SMS credits that are deducted whenever an SMS is sent. Schema: `Tenant.sms_credits`, `SmsPackage`, `SmsTransaction` (ledger) + migration `20260612010000_add_sms_credits`; `SmsCreditService` (segment-aware billing, atomic `consume`, purchase/confirm) + `SmsCreditController` (`GET /sms-credits/summary`, `POST /sms-credits/purchase`, `POST /sms-credits/confirm`); `SmsService.sendSms` now deducts credits per recipient×segment and skips sending when out of balance; wired into sale receipts, low-stock alerts, and CRM campaigns; seed adds 4 packages + 1,000 starter credits; frontend balance/top-up/history page at `/dashboard/sms-credits` + sidebar link — done 2026-06-12

- [x] Feedback automation pipeline (admin-driven: propose plan -> approve -> implement -> PR -> rollback) — new `feedback-automation` backend module. `Feedback` gains a `status` state machine (NEW/ADMIN_REVIEWING/PLAN_REQUESTED/PLAN_PROPOSED/PLAN_APPROVED/CHANGES_REQUESTED/IN_PROGRESS/PR_OPENED/MERGED/RESOLVED/ROLLED_BACK), `adminInstruction`, PR/commit/backup/error tracking fields; new versioned `FeedbackPlan` table (migration `20260705150000_add_feedback_automation`). `FeedbackAgentRunnerService` runs a Claude tool-use loop (list_dir/read_file/search_code, +write_file in implement mode) against a disposable git clone, reusing the existing OpenRouter credential (`ai.api_key`) rather than provisioning a separate Anthropic key — read-only in plan mode, and even in implement mode the model never runs git or opens the PR itself, only writes files. `FeedbackGithubService` handles clone/branch/commit/push (credentials via a scratch `.netrc`, never in argv) and PR open/revert through the GitHub REST API against a repo-scoped token (GitHub App or fine-grained PAT, configured by the admin — provisioning the actual GitHub App/token is a manual step outside this codebase). `FeedbackAutomationService` orchestrates: plan requests and implementation both run fire-and-forget in the background (endpoints return immediately) to avoid long-running-request timeouts; migration sign-off is gated at plan-approval time via the model's self-reported `MIGRATION_REQUIRED` flag (`confirmMigration` required in the review DTO when `hasMigration` + `require_migration_signoff` setting), with a second static-keyword scan (`DROP COLUMN`/`DROP TABLE`/`ALTER COLUMN ... TYPE`/`TRUNCATE`) as a belt-and-suspenders abort-before-push check in case the plan under-reported it. Rollback opens a `git revert` PR against the merged commit — deliberately never an automated DB restore, since that would roll back every tenant's data, not just one feedback item's. New `feedback_automation` platform-settings group (enabled/schedule/model/require_migration_signoff/github_token/github_repo/github_base_branch); hourly cron (`JOB_NAMES.FEEDBACK_PLAN_BATCH`) only does real work when `schedule` is `daily`/`weekly` and due. Admin UI: `FeedbackAutomationPanel` modal (instruction, propose/approve/request-changes, PR link + refresh, rollback button) opened from `/admin/feedback`; new `/admin/platform-settings/feedback-automation` settings page (index-grid link only, no sidebar `NAV_REGISTRY` entry — matches the existing Add-on Modules precedent). 15 new backend unit tests covering the state machine and the destructive-migration abort path; full backend suite (1072 tests) and frontend typecheck/lint stay green. Discovered while building the settings page: `admin/platform-settings/ai/page.tsx` calls `.then(r => r.json())` on `fetchWithAuth()`, which already returns unwrapped parsed data (not a `Response`) — reinforces the pre-existing "audit other pages for fetchWithAuth misuse" item below rather than being new, but confirms `ai/page.tsx` itself is affected — done 2026-07-05
- [x] CRM Leads Service Task 1: importRows() method — `CrmLeadsService.importRows()` for bulk CSV/Excel lead import with skip/upsert modes; validates required fields (name, mobile), applies defaults for missing enums (MEDIUM priority, OTHER source, NEW status), safely handles invalid enum values with fallbacks, rejects LOST status since lost_reason cannot be imported, computes lead scores automatically via `computeLeadScore()`, returns ImportResult with created/updated/skipped counts. Also fixed pre-existing bug: Prisma enums (LeadStatus, LeadSource, LeadCategory, LeadPriority) not exported at runtime from @prisma/client; defined locally in crm-leads.dto.ts to allow @IsEnum class-validator decorators. 6 new tests all passing (create with defaults, skip duplicate, upsert duplicate, missing-field error, invalid-enum fallback, LOST rejection); 15/15 total tests in file passing; no regressions — done 2026-07-06

- [x] Fix PR #357 CI failures — the feedback-automation agent's commit had deleted the entire `packages/shared-types/navigation.ts` (493 lines) and committed four empty junk files (`hello.ts`, `test.ts`, `test2.ts`, `test-small.ts`), which broke all nav resolution (`NavScope`/`getDefaultNavLayout` became undefined) and failed 4 backend + 2 frontend test suites. Restored `navigation.ts`, removed the junk files, and applied the actually-intended change: reparented `sales.receivables.customer-payments` directly under `sales` (sortOrder 3) and dropped the `sales.receivables` subgroup from `DEFAULT_TENANT_NAV_LAYOUT`. All CI checks green — done 2026-07-11
- [x] Harden Feedback Automation against corrupt implementations — the PR #357 incident was caused by the agent's whole-file `write_file` rewrite arriving with empty `content` (a JSON-serialization failure on a large payload), which truncated `navigation.ts` to nothing and created empty junk files; the pipeline had no gate that could catch it (the `filesChanged.length === 0` check passed since files *did* change, and the destructive-SQL scan only covers `prisma/migrations/`), so a broken PR was opened and CI was the first thing to run the code. Added two guards: (1) `rejectDestructiveWrite()` in `feedback-agent-runner.service.ts`, wired into `toolWriteFile`, rejects emptying an existing non-empty file or creating an empty file with actionable error text so the model can recover mid-run; (2) a `firstEmptyChangedFile()` structural sanity gate in `feedback-automation.service.ts` that aborts before commit/push (status back to PLAN_APPROVED with a clear lastError) if any changed file was left empty/whitespace-only. Chose a fast structural gate over a full `npm ci && tsc && jest` gate because the workspace is a `--depth 1` clone with no node_modules and CI already blocks *merging* a red PR (`computePrGreen` requires all checks green) — the gap was only broken PRs being *opened*. 7 new backend tests (5 unit on the write guard, 2 on the empty-file gate); full feedback-automation suite 44/44, backend build clean — done 2026-07-11
- [x] Auto-promote dev→main + in-app production deploy — extended Feedback Automation past the dev merge. (1) `promoteDevToMain()` in `FeedbackAutomationService`, fired fire-and-forget from `mergeFeedbackPr` (both merge paths): idempotent (no-op when main is up to date, reuses an already-open dev→main PR), serialized in-process against concurrent feedback merges, opens a dev→main PR and merges it once `getPrReadiness` reports green (polls ~15 min, halts + audit-logs on CI failure/timeout without touching main). Only feedback-automation merges trigger it; manual dev commits don't. (2) New `admin/deploy` controller (`PlatformAdminGuard`): `POST` dispatches the existing `deploy-vps.yml` GitHub workflow for the production branch (external runner, so the backend never kills its own container mid-deploy); `GET /status` returns `{ liveSha, mainSha, aheadBy, lastRun }`. `liveSha` comes from a `GIT_SHA` build-arg baked into the backend image (Dockerfile ARG/ENV + compose build arg + `deploy.sh` `git rev-parse HEAD`), also surfaced on `/health`. New `FeedbackGithubService` methods: `compareBranches`, `findOpenPullRequest`, `triggerDeployWorkflow`, `getLatestDeployRun`, `getBranches` + `github_production_branch` config. (3) `PlatformDeployPanel` UI at `/admin/platform-settings/deploy` (index-grid link): live-vs-main SHA + commits-ahead + last-run status, "Deploy to Production" button (confirm-gated, polls while a run is active). Production deploy needs the GitHub token to add `actions:write`; documented on the automation settings page. 14 new tests (5 promotion, 3 deploy-service, 6 frontend panel); backend 1487/1487, frontend platform-settings suites + build + lint clean. Design doc: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-11-auto-promote-and-deploy-design.md`. Scope note: a promotion carries the whole dev branch to main (feedback merge is only the trigger); deploy stays human-gated so nothing ships unreviewed — done 2026-07-11
- [x] Fix: dev→main auto-promotion never fired on externally-merged feedback PRs — root cause (found via prod backend logs + GitHub state): the promotion trigger lived only in `mergeFeedbackPr` (the panel's "Merge PR" button), but a feedback item also reaches MERGED via `refreshPrStatus` — the panel's poller flips it when it detects the PR was merged on GitHub directly (the panel's ExternalLink → merge on github.com). Since PRs in this repo are routinely merged on GitHub, the poller path was the common case and it had no `triggerPromotion()`, so `promoteDevToMain()` never ran (confirmed: no promotion PR, and no "dev→main promotion failed" error in backend logs). Fix: call `triggerPromotion()` from the merged-transition branch in `refreshPrStatus` too; the existing `status !== 'MERGED'` guard makes it fire exactly once per feedback, and `promoteDevToMain` is already idempotent + serialized. All 3 MERGED-transition sites now trigger promotion. 3 new tests; feedback-automation suite 56/56, backend build clean — done 2026-07-12

Commit `e806791` — done 2026-07-12
- [x] Fix custom-fields review findings: `saveDefinitions` freeSlots ignored active state — a save that kept some fields, dropped an active field, and added a new keyless field could assign the new field the dropped field's slot (e.g. `cf_2`), causing the upsert to rename that row in place instead of deactivating it, silently leaking every lead's stored value under that key into the unrelated new field. Fixed by excluding both resent keys and currently-active existing keys from `freeSlots`. Also added validation rejecting any client-supplied `key` not in `CUSTOM_FIELD_SLOTS` (previously unvalidated, breaking the fixed-slot invariant). Added 2 tests (mixed keep/drop/add reconciliation asserting `cf_2` is deactivated and not reused; invalid key rejection). `custom-fields.service.spec.ts` 6/6 GREEN, backend `tsc --noEmit` clean for custom-fields — done 2026-07-12
- [x] Fix CRM lead CSV import review finding: custom-field header matching not truly case-insensitive — `castRow`'s lookup (`raw[def.label] ?? raw[def.label.toLowerCase()]`) only matched the exact label or fully-lowercased label, so headers like `REGION`/`reGion`/`Region ` (trailing whitespace) silently failed to match and the value was dropped with no error. Fixed by normalizing both the definition label and every row key via `.trim().toLowerCase()` and scanning `Object.keys(raw)` for a match. Added test proving an uppercase `REGION` header maps into `custom_fields.cf_1` (fails against old two-form lookup). `crm-leads.service.spec.ts` 22/22 GREEN, `tsc --noEmit` clean for crm-leads. - [x] Super admin password silently reset to `password123` on every container restart — root cause was two compounding problems, both fixed. (1) `packages/database/prisma/seed.ts` put `passwordHash` in the **`update`** branch of the `nayeem.ahmad@gmail.com` upsert (introduced in `076f2a2`, 2026-03-22), so re-seeding overwrote the stored hash of the already-existing super admin; the two users directly below it correctly used `update: {}`, which is why only that one account was affected. (2) `db:seed` — the full dev/demo fixture script — was baked into the backend container's `CMD` (`apps/backend/Dockerfile`), so it ran on every deploy AND every unattended restart (crash, OOM, host reboot), writing business data (fake tenants, stores, products, sales, the demo sandbox) into production. Fix establishes the rule "prod automatically gets schema + reference data only; everything else is deliberate": split the platform catalog (5 subscription plans + 2 addon modules — genuine reference data read at runtime by `auth.service.ts`, `billing.service.ts`, `billing-scheduler.service.ts`, `admin-tenants.service.ts`, `addon-modules.service.ts`) out into new `packages/database/prisma/seed-platform.ts`, exposed as `db:seed:platform`; `seed.ts` now imports it (one definition, no drift) and gained an `assertNotProduction()` backstop that throws on `NODE_ENV=production` unless `ALLOW_PROD_SEED=1`; `Dockerfile` CMD and `scripts/deploy-erp71.sh` switched to the platform seed; `update` on the admin upsert no longer touches `passwordHash`. `.github/workflows/deploy.yaml:156` deliberately left on `db:seed` — that's the E2E job against an ephemeral CI Postgres with `NODE_ENV: test`, and Playwright needs the fixtures. Verified against local Postgres: platform seed runs clean standalone and under `NODE_ENV=production`; fixture seed refuses under `NODE_ENV=production`; and a sentinel hash written to the admin row survived a full re-seed, while temporarily restoring the old `update: { name, passwordHash }` line clobbered it back to a bcrypt hash (counterfactual confirmed both directions). Pre-existing and untouched: 4 `Record<string, unknown>` → `InputJsonValue` type errors moved verbatim into `seed-platform.ts` (confirmed present at HEAD; never surface because `tsx` transpiles without typechecking) — done 2026-07-23

- [x] **URL shortener + public share links** — design approved, spec at `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-04-url-shortener-design.md`, implemented across 13 tasks (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-08-04-url-shortener/`, branch `claude/url-shortening-feature-da20e9`). Task 1 (`isSafeTarget()` validator, `apps/backend/src/short-links/is-safe-target.ts`), Task 2 (schema — `ShortLink` model + `Quotation.share_token`/`share_token_at`, migration `20260804200000_add_short_links`), Task 3 (service layer — code generator and `ShortLinksService`), Task 4 (HTTP surface — `ShortLinksController` public resolver + tenant CRUD, `ShortLinksAdminController`, `ShortLinksModule` registered in `app.module.ts`, `MANAGE_SHORT_LINKS` permission), and Task 5 (quotation share endpoints + sanitized public DTO — `apps/backend/src/sales-quotations/public-quotation.dto.ts` with `toPublicQuotation()` as an explicit allow-list backed by an exact-key-set test; `SalesQuotationsService.share`/`revokeShare`/`findByShareToken`; `POST`/`DELETE /sales-quotations/:id/share`, with the `DELETE .../:id/share` route declared ahead of the pre-existing `DELETE .../:id` so the general route can't swallow it; unauthenticated `GET /public/quotations/:token` via `PublicQuotationsController`, resolving tenant from the token rather than from the caller). Task 4 also had to touch the DB-side `StorePermission` enum and its backfill reconciler, which the brief didn't call out: adding `MANAGE_SHORT_LINKS` to `packages/shared-types` alone left `packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma`'s mirror enum out of sync (broke `tsc` on `seed-demo.ts`), so it was added there too with migration `20260805000000_add_manage_short_links_permission` and `prisma generate` re-run; separately, per the documented lesson in `sync-role-permissions.ts` (this has silently starved Manager of a new permission three times before — lead taxonomy, `APPROVE_VOUCHER`, Projects), a `short-links` group was added to `PERMISSION_BACKFILL_GROUPS` so existing tenants' Manager role actually receives the grant on next deploy, not just newly-created tenants. Task 5 tightened the brief's own example one step further: the brief's `findByShareToken` used bare `include` on the `items` relation, but every relation now goes through `select` (down to `quantity`/`unit_price`/`product.name` only), since `QuotationItem`/`Product` carry internal ids and planning fields (`reorder_level`, `safety_stock`, `lead_time_days`) that a public page has no business loading into memory even though the DTO allow-list would already have kept them out of the response. Task 2's migration is **not yet applied to the local dev DB** — blocked on pre-existing unrelated drift (missing `Customer.[tenant_id,user_id]` unique constraint from commit `5665ab9`, 2026-05-22) that made `prisma db push` refuse without `--accept-data-loss`; only `prisma generate` was run locally so the client compiles, see `task-2-report.md` for detail (tracked separately below). Task 6 (`/s` resolver route + interstitial — `apps/frontend/src/app/s/[code]/route.ts` POSTs to `/short-links/resolve/:code` to resolve-and-count, 302s straight through for `kind: 'internal'`, and for `kind: 'external'` redirects to `/s/[code]/leaving` rather than passing the target in a query param; that interstitial page independently GETs the same resolve endpoint server-side — no click counted — so the host it displays and the host it sends you to can never diverge, and any resolve failure/non-external kind hits `notFound()`). Task 7 (public quotation view — `apps/frontend/src/app/q/[token]/page.tsx` server-fetches `GET /public/quotations/:token` and `PublicQuotationView.tsx` renders it; a non-OK response or a body with no `quote_number` both render the identical `Unavailable` component, so a missing token and a revoked one are indistinguishable; every money value — unit price, line total, grand total — goes through `formatBDT`; the line-items table is wrapped in its own `overflow-x-auto` so a long product name can't force horizontal body scroll at 360px). Task 8 (public storefront product page — `GET /storefront/:slug/products/:productId` selecting only customer-facing `Product` fields via `findEnabledTenant()`, and `apps/frontend/src/app/store/[slug]/p/[productId]/page.tsx` mirroring Task 7's failure-handling pattern byte-for-byte). Task 9 (share modal and call sites — `ShareModal` + wiring into the quotation detail page). Task 10 (navigation, routes and translations — `admin.url-shortener` and `account-settings.url-shortener` registered in `NAV_REGISTRY`, `admin.url-shortener` shipped in both `DEFAULT_PLATFORM_ADMIN_NAV_LAYOUT` and `DEFAULT_TENANT_NAV_LAYOUT`'s `admin` block, `routes.admin.urlShortener`/`routes.settings.urlShortener` added, `en`/`bn`/`ms` translations added). Task 11 (shared `ShortLinkManager` component — `apps/frontend/src/components/short-links/ShortLinkManager.tsx` — plus the platform admin page at `/admin/url-shortener`). Task 12 (tenant `/settings/url-shortener` page reusing the same component, plus the `/settings` hub card). Task 13 — closes the product gap the code review surfaced: a shared quotation that got revised kept serving the superseded pricing forever because `share_token` stayed on the old row. `SalesQuotationsService.revise()` (`apps/backend/src/sales-quotations/sales-quotations.service.ts`) now, inside the existing `$transaction`: clears `share_token`/`share_token_at` on the old row in the same `update` that marks it `REVISED` (must precede the create — Postgres checks the `@unique` constraint per statement, not deferred), carries both fields onto the new row in the `create`, and re-points any live `ShortLink` (`entity_type: 'QUOTATION'`, `revoked_at: null`) at the new quotation id via `updateMany` so re-sharing after a revision reuses the existing code instead of minting a redundant one; return value unchanged (still the new quotation with items). 6 new tests in `sales-quotations.service.spec.ts` (token moves; old row cleared; clear precedes create — pinned via `mock.invocationCallOrder`, counterfactual-verified by temporarily reordering the implementation to confirm the test goes RED; unshared quotation unaffected; short link re-pointed; `updateMany` matching zero rows doesn't throw), plus one pre-existing `revise()` test updated for the now-cleared token fields in its `update` assertion. `npx jest src/sales-quotations` 32/32 passing; `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.build.json` clean (no output). **The shortener is the small half** — quotations are authenticated CRUD by default, so `/q/[token]` and `/s/[code]` are what make "share a quotation over WhatsApp" possible at all. `isSafeTarget()` remains the other major risk (external URLs are allowed, so this is what stops `app.erp71.com/s/xyz` becoming a phishing vector — backed by an interstitial on off-domain targets so even a validation miss cannot produce a silent redirect). Accept/Reject on the public quotation, vanity codes, and link expiry remain deliberately out of scope — done 2026-08-05
- [x] **URL shortener: whole-branch review fix wave** — one consolidated pass over the eight findings from the final code review of `claude/url-shortening-feature-da20e9`. **(1) Every public URL 404'd in production.** All four new server-side fetches (plus the pre-existing `/r/[code]` referral route) built their API base as `configured || 'http://localhost:4000/api/v1'` — the fallback carried the `/api/v1` prefix, the configured value did not, and `scripts/sync-erp71-env-urls.sh` forces `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=https://api.erp71.com` (bare origin) on every deploy while the backend sets `app.setGlobalPrefix('api/v1')`. Because every path fails closed, the whole public half of the feature surfaced to users as "this link is no longer available" rather than as a misconfiguration; referral click tracking had been a silent no-op in production for the same reason. Extracted `apps/frontend/src/lib/api-base.ts` (`normalizeApiBase`, `publicApiBase`), which `lib/api.ts` now also imports so there is exactly one implementation of the rule; deleted all five copy-pasted `apiBase()` helpers. The existing guard test asserted `/api/v1` appeared *at most* once — satisfied by zero, which is why this shipped — now `exactly` once, plus dedicated tests on `/s`, `/r`, `/q` and the storefront product page that set a prefix-less base; counterfactually verified RED against the pre-fix behaviour. **(2) Revocation had no UI.** `api.revokeQuotationShare` had zero call sites, so with no token expiry a shared quotation was permanently public. Added a Revoke action to `ShareModal` behind a two-step in-modal confirmation (not `confirm()`), failures via the Toaster, modal stays open on failure so a failed revoke never reads as a successful one. **(3) `revokeShare()` left the `ShortLink` row live** — `/s/<code>` kept resolving and counting clicks onto a dead `/q/<token>`, the tenant's own shortener page still showed the link active with a Revoke button, and re-sharing orphaned the old row. Now sets `revoked_at` on matching rows in the same `$transaction` as the token clear. **(4) The public product page quoted a different price than the shop page** — `getPublicProduct` returned `Number(product.price)` raw while `getStorefront` resolves every listed price through `priceListsService.getResolvedPricesForProducts()`. Now resolved through the same resolver against the same anonymous-visitor price list; chose to *show availability* (`in_stock` boolean, never the quantity) rather than filter out-of-stock products, since a shared link can outlive availability and 404ing a live product is worse than labelling it. **(5) The platform-admin list exposed every tenant's quotation share tokens** — `tenantId ? { tenant_id: tenantId } : {}` meant "all rows", so any platform staffer could open any tenant's customer quotations with full pricing, and auto-created quotation links crowded the platform's own campaign links out of `take: 200`. Now `{ tenant_id: tenantId }` in both directions (null = platform-owned, per the spec's data model); `revoke` stays deliberately unscoped and that is now documented. Stale "spans every tenant" admin copy corrected in all three locales. **(6) Public pages were indexable** — added `app/robots.ts` disallowing `/q/` and `/s/`, plus `robots: { index: false, follow: false }` metadata on the quotation page; the storefront product page stays indexable on purpose (marketing). **(7) `ShareModal` was hardcoded English** in the one modal a Bangladeshi shop owner actually uses to send a WhatsApp link — routed through `useI18n()` with real bn/ms copy, and the quotation-specific wording made generic so the storefront share can reuse it. **(8) No controller spec for the short-link HTTP surface** — guards are declared per method, so dropping one would have exposed a cross-tenant write with every test still green; `short-links.controller.spec.ts` now pins the guard/interceptor/permission metadata via `Reflect.getMetadata`, that both resolve routes stay public, and that `GET` resolves without counting while `POST` counts. Promoted minors: stable `orderBy: { id: 'asc' }` on public quotation line items (row order on a customer-facing printed document could vary between loads), and the sanitized DTO's headline comment corrected — it claimed a new `Quotation` column "must fail the accompanying test", which the allow-list makes impossible; reworded to what the test actually pins (the exact output key set), in the DTO, its spec, and the design spec. Verification: backend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.build.json` clean, `jest src/short-links src/sales-quotations src/storefront src/navigation` 226/226; frontend `tsc --noEmit` clean but for the two known pre-existing errors (stale `.next` entry for the renamed `crm/tasks` page; `contact/page.test.tsx` importing the uninstalled `@testing-library/user-event`), `jest` 2220/2220 across 188 suites — done 2026-08-05

Always 12 buckets, oldest first, ending with the current month; empty months present with zeros rather than being omitted; each series buckets on its own date field (clicks→`occurred_at`, signups→`signed_up_at`, earned→`earned_at`, paid→`paid_at`); `REVERSED` never counts as earned, mirroring `getLedger`'s existing rule; rows older than the window are dropped, not folded into the first bucket. 8 tests, `npx jest src/referrals/referral-activity` 8/8. **Task 2** — `ReferralsService.getLedger` gained `activity` (via `buildActivity`) and `payments[].commissions` (the same join `listPayments` already did). `summary.clicks` stays the all-time count from the existing `referralClick.count`; the 12-month buckets get their own bounded `referralClick.findMany` selecting only `occurred_at` — a `groupBy` can't bucket by month (it would group per distinct timestamp) and `$queryRaw`+`date_trunc` would cost testability against the mocked `db`. The `describe('getLedger')` `beforeEach` gained default mocks for the two new/changed queries because `jest.resetAllMocks()` would otherwise return `undefined` and break the seven pre-existing tests. 5 new tests; `npx jest src/referrals` 103/103. **Task 4** — `/referrals/signups` and `/referrals/payments` as their own pages, the shared `useRefereeLedger()` hook (all three portal pages now read the one ledger endpoint through it), and `PaymentDetailModal`. Signups adds Plan amount / Commission % / Earned on columns and status filter presets; payments adds Notes and a `ModalShell` detail listing the commissions each payout settled — a modal rather than row expansion because `DataTable` has no expansion support and adding it would change every list screen in the product. i18n added verbatim to en/bn/ms. **Code review caught one thing:** `meta: { hideOnMobile: true }` had been removed from the payments `reference` column to make a test pass under jsdom's `matchMedia` mock (always `matches: false`, so `useIsMdUp()` goes false and `DataTable` genuinely drops those columns) — reverted to restore production behaviour (parity with the signups page's own `hideOnMobile` columns, no reintroduced 360px horizontal-scroll risk) and fixed the test instead with the `@/hooks/useMediaQuery` mock the codebase already uses in `Sidebar.test.tsx` and the sales customers page. **Task 5** — `apps/frontend/src/components/referrals/`: `chart-theme.ts`, `funnel-model.ts`, `ActivityChart`, `EarningsChart`, `FunnelChart`, hand-rolled SVG following the existing `CashFlowChart` precedent (this repo has no charting library). Exactly two hues, blue `#2563eb` and emerald `#047857`, validated with the dataviz palette validator against both light and dark chart surfaces — deutan separation ΔE 23.6, normal-vision ΔE 25.4, but tritan separation is ΔE 7.5, inside the 6–8 floor band, so every multi-series chart also carries a legend and direct labels rather than relying on hue alone. `ActivityChart` is two stacked panels sharing one x-axis rather than a dual y-axis, because clicks and signups differ by an order of magnitude and a second y-scale would make incomparable scales look comparable. Funnel stages are cumulative and drop-off renders as an em dash, never `NaN`, when the previous stage is zero. 13 tests; `npx jest src/components/referrals` 4 suites/13 tests. **Verified across the four tasks:** backend `npx jest src/referrals` 103/103; frontend `npx jest src/components/referrals src/components/Sidebar.test.tsx "src/app/\(app\)/referrals"` 8 suites/48 tests, full frontend suite 204 suites/2355 tests green; locale catalogue parity green in all three; both typechecks at the known pre-existing baseline (one unrelated `@testing-library/user-event` error, confirmed via `git stash` A/B).
